Over the past few years, we have seen a period of intense crisis of survival for        在過去幾年，我們看到香港和世界許多地方的

challenges of the post-COVID Era, geopolitical tensions, military occupation,           軍事佔領、供應鏈中斷、高利息和通貨膨脹率
supply chain disruptions, high interest and inflation rate, and other upheavals         以及其他在近代歷史上很少遇到的動盪中改
that were rarely encountered in recent history. The Group remained resilient and        進、重新評估和重新定位自己的業務。本集團
focused on its existing businesses.                                                     保持彈性，並專注於其現有業務。

b)   Credit risk assessment policy                                                  (b)    信貸風險評估政策

     The Group has performed background and credit risk assessment on the                  授出貸款之前，本集團已對潛在借款人進
     potential borrowers before granting the loans by (a) searching on their               行背景及信貸風險評估，包括(a)對其身份
     identity and background; (b) reviewing and assessing their financial                  及背景進行搜索；(b)審查及評估其財務信
     information; and (c) performing an assessment on their creditability.                 息；及(c)對其信用度進行評估。

     The Group has adopted a credit policy to manage its money lending                     本集團已採納信貸政策管理其放貸業務，
     business which includes compliance with all applicable laws and                       包括遵守所有適用法律及法規、對潛在借
     regulations, credit assessment on potential borrower and his/its assets,              款人以及其資產、潛在借款人的可信程度

Interest rate risk                                                                     利率風險

The Group’s pledged bank deposits and finance lease receivables bear fixed             本集團之已抵押銀行存款及融資租賃應收款項
interest rates. The Group’s cash at bank balances bear floating interest rates. The    以固定利率計息。本集團之銀行現金結餘以浮
Group also has borrowings and obligation under finance leases and an interest          動利率計息。本集團亦有借貸及融資租賃責
free loan from a director. Exposure to interest rate risk exists on those balances     任，及來自一名董事之免息貸款。倘有未能預
subject to floating interest rate when there are unexpected adverse interest rate      料的不利利率變動，該等以浮動利率計息之結
movements. The Group’s policy is to manage its interest rate risk, working within      餘將面對利率風險。本集團的政策為在協定之
an agreed framework, to ensure that there are no undue exposures to significant        框架內管理其利率風險，以確保不會面對利率
interest rate movements and rates are appropriately fixed when necessary.              大幅變動此不合理風險，並於有需要時適當地
                                                                                       固定利率。

Currency risk                                                                          貨幣風險

The Group mainly operates in Hong Kong with most of the transactions                   本集團於香港營業，大部分交易以港元、美
denominated and settled in HK$, US$, JPY and EUR. The Group’s exposure to              元、日元及歐元計值及結算。本集團面對的外
foreign currency risk primarily arises from certain financial instruments including    幣風險主要來自以美元及歐元計值之若干金融
trade receivables, bank balances and cash, trade payables, borrowings and              工具，包括貿易應收款項、銀行結餘及現金、
obligation under finance leases which are denominated in US$ and EUR. The              貿易應付款項、借貸及融資租賃責任。本集團
Group does not adopt any hedging strategy in the long run but the management           並無採納任何長遠對沖策略，但管理層持續監
continuously monitors the foreign exchange risk exposure and might enter into          察外匯風險並可能按個別情況訂立遠期匯兌合
foreign exchange forward contracts on a case-by-case basis. The Group has not          約。本集團並無採用任何對沖合約以從事投機
used any hedging contracts to engage in speculative activities.                        活動。

Credit risk and liquidity risk                                                         信貸風險及流動資金風險

The Group has adopted a prudent financial management approach towards its              本集團的庫務政策已採取審慎的財務管理方
treasury policies and thus maintained a healthy liquidity position throughout the      針，故已在本年度維持穩健的流動資金狀況。
Year. The Group strives to reduce exposure to credit risk by performing ongoing        本集團致力透過進行持續的信貸評估及判斷其
credit assessments and evaluations of the financial status of its customers. To        客戶的財務狀況降低信貸風險。為管理流動資
manage liquidity risk, the Board closely monitors the Group’s liquidity position       金風險，董事會密切監察本集團的流動資金狀
to ensure that the liquidity structure of the Group’s assets, liabilities and other    況，以確保本集團的資產、負債及其他承擔的
commitments can meet its funding requirements.                                         流動資金架構符合其資金要求。

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                                         企業管治常規
The Company and the Board are devoted to achieving and maintaining high                本公司及董事會致力達致及維持最高水平之企
standard of corporate governance as the Board believes that effective corporate        業管治，董事會相信，行之有效的企業管治常
governance practices are fundamental in enhancing the shareholder value                規對提升股東價值以及保障股東及其他權益相
and safeguarding the interests of the Shareholders and other stakeholders.             關者之利益至關重要。因此，本公司已採納健
Accordingly, the Company has adopted sound corporate governance principles             全之企業管治原則，當中著重優秀之董事會、
that emphasise a quality Board, effective internal control, stringent disclosure       有效之內部監控、嚴謹之披露常規以及對所有
practices and transparency and accountability to all Shareholders.                     股東之透明度及問責性。

The Company has complied with the code provisions set out in the Corporate             本公司已遵守上市規則附錄十四所載之企業管
Governance Code (the “CG Code”) contained in Appendix 14 to the Listing                治守則（
                                                                                          「企業管治守則」   ）之守則條文，惟偏離守
Rules, except for the deviation from the code provision C.2.1.                         則條文第C.2.1條除外。

Under code provision C.2.1, the roles of chairman and chief executive should           根據守則條文第C.2.1條，主席與行政總裁之角
be separate and should not be performed by the same individual in order to             色應有區分，並不應由同一名人士兼任，以確
ensure that there is clear division of responsibilities between the chairman of the    保董事會主席與本公司行政總裁之職責有明確
Board and the chief executive of the Company. The Company has not appointed            區分。本公司並無委任主席，而董事會則領導
a chairman and the Board provides leadership for the Company. Having                   本公司。考慮到本集團於重大時刻之業務營
considered the business operation of the Group at the material time, it is believed    運，本公司認為董事會由經驗豐富之專業人士
that the Board, which consists of experienced professionals, can function              組成，整體可有效運作，而執行董事連同本公
effectively as a whole, while the executive Directors along with other members         司其他高級管理人員則有能力監督本集團在現
of senior management of the Company are capable of overseeing the day-to-day           有穩固企業管治架構下之日常管理工作。
management of the Group under the strong corporate governance structure in
place.

The Company nevertheless understands the importance of compliance with the             儘管如此，本公司明白遵守企業管治守則之守
code provision C.2.1 of the CG Code and will continue to review the structure          則條文第C.2.1條十分重要，並將繼續不時檢討
from time to time and consider appointing a chairman/chairlady of the Board as         有關架構，亦考慮在適當時委任董事會主席。
and when appropriate.

The Group commits to continuously improving its corporate governance practices         本集團承諾透過定期檢討持續改善其企業管治
by periodic review to ensure that the Group continues to meet the requirements         常規以確保本集團繼續達到企業管治守則之要
of the CG Code.                                                                        求。

Summary of the Board Diversity Policy                                                 董事會多元化政策摘要

With a view to achieving a sustainable and balanced development, the Company          為達致可持續均衡發展，本公司視董事會層面
sees increasing diversity at the Board level as an essential element in supporting    日益多元化為支持其達到策略目標及可持續發

age, cultural and educational background, professional experience, skills and         驗、技能以及知識。董事會所有委任均以用人
knowledge. All Board appointments will be based on merit, and candidates will         唯才為原則，並在考慮人選時以客觀條件充分
be considered against objective criteria, having due regard for the benefits of       顧及董事會多元化之裨益。
diversity on the Board.

OPINION                                                                               意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Hao Tian International       本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」 ）已審計列載於第71至
Construction Investment Group Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries            171頁的昊天國際建設投資集團有限公司 （以下簡
(collectively referred to as the “Group”) set out on pages 71 to 171, which           稱「貴公司」 ）及其附屬公司（以下統稱「貴集團」 ）
comprise the consolidated statement of financial position as at 31 March 2023,        的綜合財務報表，此財務報表包括於二零二三
and the consolidated statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive income,      年三月三十一日的綜合財務狀況表與截至該日
consolidated statement of changes in equity and consolidated statement of cash        止年度的綜合損益及其他全面收益表、綜合權
flows for the year then ended, and notes to the consolidated financial statements,    益變動表和綜合現金流量表，以及綜合財務報
including a summary of significant accounting policies.                               表附註，包括主要會計政策概要。

In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and fair view       我們認為，該等綜合財務報表已根據香港會計
of the consolidated financial position of the Group as at 31 March 2023, and of       師公會（
                                                                                         「香港會計師公會」 ）頒佈的《香港財務報
its consolidated financial performance and its consolidated cash flows for the        告準則》（「香港財務報告準則」）真實而中肯地反

THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS                                                 任
Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the consolidated      我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不存
financial statements as a whole are free from material misstatement, whether due      在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取得
to fraud or error, and to issue an auditor’s report that includes our opinion. We     合理保證，並出具包括我們意見的核數師報
report our opinion solely to you, as a body, and for no other purpose. We do          告。我們僅向 閣下（作為整體）報告我們的意
not assume responsibility towards or accept liability to any other person for the     見，除此之外別無其他目的。我們並不就本報
contents of this report. Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is    告的內容對任何其他人士承擔任何責任或接受
not a guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs will always          任何義務。合理保證是高水準的保證，但不能
detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements can arise from fraud     保證按照香港審計準則進行的審計，在某一重
or error and are considered material if, individually or in the aggregate, they       大錯誤陳述存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可以由
could reasonably be expected to influence the economic decisions of users taken       欺詐或錯誤引起，如果合理預期它們單獨或匯
on the basis of these consolidated financial statements.                              總起來可能影響綜合財務報表使用者依賴綜合
                                                                                      財務報表所作出的經濟決定，則有關的錯誤陳
                                                                                      述可被視作重大。

       and Interpretations. The adoption of these new and revised HKFRSs did                   務報告準則」 ）；香港會計準則（「香港會計

       Joint arrangements                                                                        共同安排

       A joint arrangement is an arrangement of which two or more parties have                   合營安排指雙方或多方於其中擁有共同控
       joint control. Joint control is the contractually agreed sharing of control               制權之安排。共同控制是指按照合約約定
       of an arrangement, which exists only when decisions about the relevant                    對某項安排所共有之控制，共同控制僅在
       activities require the unanimous consent of the parties sharing control.                  當相關活動要求共同享有控制權之各方作
       Relevant activities are activities that significantly affect the returns of               出一致同意之決定時存在。相關活動指對
       the arrangement. When assessing joint control, the Group considers its                    安排回報有重大影響之活動。在評估共同
       potential voting rights as well as potential voting rights held by other                  控制時，本集團會考慮其潛在投票權，以
       parties, to determine whether it has joint control. A potential voting right is           及由其他方持有之潛在投票權，以釐定其
       considered only if the holder has the practical ability to exercise that right.           是否具有共同控制權。潛在投票權只有在
                                                                                                 持有人具有行使該權利之實際能力時才予
                                                                                                 以考慮。

       A joint arrangement is either a joint operation or a joint venture. A joint               合營安排指共同經營或合營企業。共同經
       operation is a joint arrangement whereby the parties that have joint                      營乃對該項安排擁有共同控制權之各方對
       control of the arrangement have rights to the assets, and obligations                     該項安排之資產擁有權利及對其負債承擔
       for the liabilities, relating to the arrangement. A joint venture is a joint              責任之合營安排。合營企業乃擁有共同控
       arrangement whereby the parties that have joint control of the arrangement                制權之各方對該項安排之淨資產擁有權利
       have rights to the net assets of the arrangement.                                         之合營安排。

       In relation to its interest in a joint operation, the Group recognises in                 有關共同經營之權益，本集團乃按照適用
       its consolidated financial statements, its assets, including its share of                 於特定資產、負債、收益及開支之香港財
       any assets held jointly; its liabilities, including its share of any liabilities          務報告準則，於其綜合財務報表中確認其
       incurred jointly; its revenue from the sale of its share of the output arising            資產（包括分佔共同持有之任何資產）；其
       from the joint operation; its share of the revenue from the sale of the                   負債（包括分佔共同產生之任何負債）；來
       output by the joint operation; and its expenses, including its share of any               自出售其共同經營所分佔產出之收益；其

       of joint control represents the difference between (i) the fair value of the           益乃指以下兩者之差異：(i)銷售代價之公
       consideration of the sale plus the fair value of any investment retained in            平值加上於該合營企業任何保留投資之公
       that joint venture and (ii) the Group’s share of the net assets of that joint          平值及(ii)本集團應佔該合營企業之資產淨
       venture plus any remaining goodwill relating to that joint venture and any             值加上有關該合營企業之剩餘商譽及任何
       related accumulated foreign currency translation reserve. If an investment             相關累計外幣換算儲備。倘於合營企業之
       in a joint venture becomes an investment in an associate, the Group                    投資成為於聯營公司之投資，則本集團繼
       continues to apply the equity method and does not remeasure the retained               續應用權益法，且不會重新計量保留權
       interest.                                                                              益。

       Unrealised profits on transactions between the Group and its joint ventures            本集團與其合營企業之間交易之未實現溢
       are eliminated to the extent of the Group’s interests in the joint ventures.           利以本集團於合營企業之權益予以抵銷。
       Unrealised losses are also eliminated unless the transaction provides                  除非有關交易提供轉讓資產之減值憑證，
       evidence of an impairment of the asset transferred. Accounting policies of             否則未實現之虧損亦予以抵銷。合營企業
       joint ventures have been changed where necessary to ensure consistency                 之會計政策在必要時已經更改，以確保與

       Loss allowances for expected credit losses                                               預期信貸虧損之虧損撥備

       The Group recognises loss allowances for expected credit losses on                       本集團按攤銷成本及融資租賃應收款項確
       financial assets at amortised cost and finance lease receivables. Expected               認金融資產之預期信貸虧損之虧損撥備。
       credit losses are the weighted average of credit losses with the respective              預期信貸虧損乃加權平均信貸虧損，並以
       risks of a default occurring as the weights.                                             發生相關違約之風險作為加權數值。

       At the end of each reporting period, the Group measures the loss                         於各報告期末或倘金融工具之信貸風險自
       allowance for a financial instrument at an amount equal to the expected                  初始確認以來大幅增加，本集團就貿易應
       credit losses that result from all possible default events over the expected             收款項，按相等於所有可能發生違約事件
       life of that financial instrument (“lifetime expected credit losses”) for trade          之預期信貸虧損除以該金融工具之預計年
       receivables or if the credit risk on that financial instrument has increased             期（
                                                                                                 「全期預期信貸虧損」
                                                                                                          ）之金額，計量金
       significantly since initial recognition.                                                 融工具之虧損撥備。

       If, at the end of the reporting period, the credit risk on a financial                   倘於報告期末金融工具（貿易應收款項除
       instrument (other than trade receivables) has not increased significantly                外）之信貸風險自初始確認以來並無大幅
       since initial recognition, the Group measures the loss allowance for that                增加，則本集團按相等於反映該金融工具
       financial instrument at an amount equal to the portion of lifetime expected              可能於報告期間後十二個月內發生之違約

       default events on that financial instrument that are possible within 12                  虧損部分之金額計量該金融工具之虧損撥
       months after the reporting period.                                                       備。

       months after the reporting period.                                                  類為流動負債。

       method unless the effect of discounting would be immaterial, in which               量，除非貼現之影響輕微，則在此情況下
       case they are stated at cost.                                                       按成本列賬。

       recognised in profit or loss.                                                       益確認。

       with a customer with reference to the customary business practices and              代價計量，且不包括代表第三方收取之款

       Revenue from contracts with customers (Continued)                                     客戶合約收入（續）

       The Group recognises revenue when it satisfies a performance obligation               本集團於透過將某項產品或服務之控制權
       by transferring control over a product or service to a customer. Depending            轉移至客戶而達成履約責任時確認收入。
       on the terms of a contract and the laws that apply to that contract, a                視乎合約條款及該合約適用之法例，履約
       performance obligation can be satisfied over time or at a point in time. A            責任可隨時間或於特定時間點達成。倘符
       performance obligation is satisfied over time if:                                     合以下條件，履約責任須隨時間達成：

              provided by the Group’s performance;                                                  用本集團履約所提供之利益；

       –      the Group’s performance creates or enhances an asset that the                  –      本集團履約產生或增強一項於產生
              customer controls as the asset is created or enhanced; or                             或增強時由客戶控制之資產；或

       –      the Group’s performance does not create an asset with an                       –      本集團履約並無產生對本集團而言
              alternative use to the Group and the Group has an enforceable right                   具替代用途之資產，且本集團對迄
              to payment for performance completed to date.                                         今完成之履約付款具有可執行權
                                                                                                    利。

       performance obligation. Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in                得產品或服務之控制權時確認。
       time when the customer obtains control of the product or service.

       Borrowing costs                                                                          借貸成本

       substantial period of time to get ready for their intended use or sale, are              貸成本，將資本化作為該等資產成本之一

       expenditure on qualifying assets is deducted from the borrowing costs                    收入須自撥充資本之借貸成本扣除。
       eligible for capitalisation.

       of obtaining a qualifying asset, the amount of borrowing costs eligible                  格資產，合資格資本化之借貸成本金額則

                                                                                                           。
       the purpose of obtaining a qualifying asset.

       Taxation                                                                                 稅項

       Income tax represents the sum of the current tax and deferred tax.                       所得稅指即期稅項及遞延稅項總額。

       The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the year. Taxable               即期應付稅項乃按本年度應課稅溢利計
       profit differs from profit recognised in profit or loss because it excludes              算。應課稅溢利不包括其他年度之應課稅
       items of income or expense that are taxable or deductible in other years                 或應扣減之收入及開支項目，亦不包括可
       and it further excludes items that are never taxable or deductible. The                  作免稅或不可作稅項扣減之項目，故與損
       Group’s liability for current tax is calculated using tax rates that have been           益所確認溢利不同。本集團之即期稅項負
       enacted or substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.                     債按其於報告期末前已制定或實質制定之
                                                                                                稅率計算。

       Deferred tax is recognised on differences between the carrying amounts                    遞延稅項就綜合財務報表內資產及負債賬
       of assets and liabilities in the consolidated financial statements and                    面值與計算應課稅溢利所採用相應稅基之
       the corresponding tax bases used in the computation of taxable profit.                    差額予以確認。遞延稅項負債一般按所有
       Deferred tax liabilities are generally recognised for all taxable temporary               應課稅暫時性差額確認，而遞延稅項資產
       differences and deferred tax assets are recognised to the extent that it is               則於很可能對銷應課稅溢利應用可扣減暫
       probable that taxable profits will be available against which deductible                  時差額之情況下才予以確認，未動用稅項
       temporary differences, unused tax losses or unused tax credits can be                     虧損或未動用稅項抵免均可動用。倘暫時
       utilised. Such assets and liabilities are not recognised if the temporary                 差額由商譽或初次確認一項不影響應課稅
       difference arises from goodwill or from the initial recognition (other than               溢利或會計溢利之交易之其他資產及負債
       in a business combination) of other assets and liabilities in a transaction              （業務合併除外）所產生，則有關資產及負
       that affects neither the taxable profit nor the accounting profit.                        債不予以確認。

       Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary differences                遞延稅項負債就於附屬公司之投資所產生
       arising on investments in subsidiaries, except where the Group is able to                應課稅暫時差額予以確認，惟倘本集團有
       control the reversal of the temporary difference and it is probable that the             能力控制暫時差額撥回，而暫時差額很可
       temporary difference will not reverse in the foreseeable future.                         能將不會於可見將來撥回則除外。

       The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at the end of each                遞延稅項資產之賬面值乃於各報告期末進
       reporting period and reduced to the extent that it is no longer probable that            行檢討，並予以相應扣減，直至並無足夠
       sufficient taxable profits will be available to allow all or part of the asset to        應課稅溢利可供全部或部分資產可予收回
       be recovered.                                                                            為止。

       Deferred tax is calculated at the tax rates that are expected to apply in the            遞延稅項根據於報告期末前已制定或實質
       period when the liability is settled or the asset is realised, based on tax              制定之稅率，按預期適用於負債清償或資
       rates that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the end of the                  產變現期間之稅率計算。遞延稅項於損益
       reporting period. Deferred tax is recognised in profit or loss, except when              中確認，除非遞延稅項關乎於其他全面收
       it relates to items recognised in other comprehensive income or directly                 益或直接於權益中予以確認之項目，在該
       in equity, in which case the deferred tax is also recognised in other                    情況下，遞延稅項亦於其他全面收益或直
       comprehensive income or directly in equity.                                              接於權益中予以確認。

       The measurement of deferred tax assets and liabilities reflects the tax                  遞延稅項資產及負債之計量反映按本集團
       consequences that would follow from the manner in which the Group                        預計於報告期末收回或清償其資產及負債
       expects, at the end of the reporting period, to recover or settle the carrying           之賬面值之稅務後果。
       amount of its assets and liabilities.

3.     SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                                   3.     主要會計政策（續）
       (CONTINUED)

       Impairment of assets                                                                     資產減值

       Intangible assets that have an indefinite useful life or not yet available for           具有無限可使用年期或尚未可供使用之無
       use are reviewed annually for impairment and are reviewed for impairment                 形資產會每年進行減值檢討，並於發生任
       whenever events or changes in circumstances indicate the carrying                        何事件或情況有變顯示未必能夠收回賬面
       amount may not be recoverable.                                                           值時檢討是否減值。

       At the end of each reporting period, the Group reviews the carrying                      於各報告期末，本集團會對其有形及其他
       amounts of its tangible and other intangible assets except goodwill,                     無形資產（商譽、遞延稅項資產、存貨及
       deferred tax assets, inventories and receivables to determine whether there              應收款項除外）之賬面值進行檢討，以釐
       is any indication that those assets have suffered an impairment loss. If any             定有否任何跡象顯示該等資產出現減值虧
       such indication exists, the recoverable amount of the asset is estimated                 損。倘出現任有關跡象，則會估計該項資
       in order to determine the extent of any impairment loss. Where it is not                 產之可收回金額以釐定任何減值虧損之程
       possible to estimate the recoverable amount of an individual asset, the                  度。倘未能估計個別資產之可收回金額，
       Group estimates the recoverable amount of cash- generating units (“CGU”)                 則本集團會估計該項資產所屬之現金產生
       to which the asset belongs.                                                              單位
                                                                                                 （「現金產生單位」
                                                                                                         ）之可收回金額。

       Recoverable amount is the higher of fair value less costs of disposal and                可收回金額為公平值減銷售成本及其使用
       value in use. In assessing value in use, the estimated future cash flows are             價值兩者中之較高者。評估使用價值時，
       discounted to their present value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects            估計未來現金流量以除稅前貼現率貼現至
       current market assessments of the time value of money and the risks                      彼等之現值，而該貼現率反映當時市場對
       specific to the asset.                                                                   金錢時間價值之評估及該項資產之特有風
                                                                                                險。

       If the recoverable amount of an asset or CGU is estimated to be less than                倘資產或現金產生單位之可收回金額估計
       its carrying amount, the carrying amount of the asset or CGU is reduced to               低於其賬面值，則將該資產或現金產生單
       its recoverable amount. An impairment loss is recognised immediately in                  位之賬面值減低至其可收回金額。減值虧
       profit or loss, unless the relevant asset is carried at a revalued amount, in            損會於損益內即時確認，除非有關資產按
       which case the impairment loss is treated as a revaluation decrease.                     重估價值列賬，在該情況下，減值虧損則
                                                                                                視作重估減少處理。
       Where an impairment loss subsequently reverses, the carrying amount of
       the asset or CGU is increased to the revised estimate of its recoverable                 倘減值虧損於其後撥回，則將該資產或現
       amount, but so that the increased carrying amount does not exceed the                    金產生單位之賬面值增加至其重新估算之
       carrying amount that would have been determined (net of amortisation                     可收回金額，惟增加後之賬面值不能超過
       or depreciation) had no impairment loss been recognised for the asset                    該資產或現金產生單位過往年度已確認為
       or CGU in prior years. A reversal of an impairment loss is recognised                    無減值虧損之賬面值（扣除攤銷或折舊）。
       immediately in profit or loss, unless the relevant asset is carried at a                 減值虧損之撥回會於損益內即時確認，除
       revalued amount, in which case the reversal of the impairment loss is                    非有關資產按重估價值列賬，在該情況
       treated as a revaluation increase.                                                       下，減值虧損撥回則視作重估增加處理。

       Provisions and contingent liabilities                                                撥備及或然負債

       Provisions are recognised for liabilities of uncertain timing or amount              倘本集團須就已發生之事件承擔現有法律
       when the Group has a present legal or constructive obligation arising as             或推定責任，而且履行責任可能涉及經濟
       a result of a past event, it is probable that an outflow of economic benefits        效益之流出，並可作出可靠之估計，則須
       will be required to settle the obligation and a reliable estimate can be             就不肯定時間或數額之負債確認撥備。倘
       made. Where the time value of money is material, provisions are stated at            金錢之時間價值重大，則撥備將會以預計
       the present value of the expenditures expected to settle the obligation.             履行責任之支出現值列示。

       Where it is not probable that an outflow of economic benefits will be                倘不大可能涉及經濟效益之流出，或無法
       required, or the amount cannot be estimated reliably, the obligation is              作出可靠之估計，則將責任披露為或然負
       disclosed as a contingent liability, unless the probability of outflow is            債，惟經濟效益流出之可能性極低則除
       remote. Possible obligations, whose existence will only be confirmed by              外。須視乎某項或多項未來事件是否發生
       the occurrence or non-occurrence of one or more future events are also               才能確定存在與否之可能責任，亦會披露
       disclosed as contingent liabilities unless the probability of outflow is             為或然負債，惟經濟效益流出之可能性極
       remote.                                                                              低則除外。

       Events after the reporting period                                                    報告期後事項

       Events after the reporting period that provide additional information                報告期後事項若與提供有關本集團於報告
       about the Group’s position at the end of the reporting period or those that          期末狀況之額外資料，或顯示其持續經營
       indicate the going concern assumption is not appropriate are adjusting               之假設並不適用，則該報告期後事項為調
       events and are reflected in the consolidated financial statements. Events            整事項，並於綜合財務報表反映。不屬調
       after the reporting period that are not adjusting events are disclosed in the        整事項之報告期後事項如屬重大時，則於
       notes when material.                                                                 附註內披露。

              Group has no significant influence over Kingdom Future and it is                    力，故將帝暉入賬為以公平值計量

6.     FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENTS                                                         6.     公平值計量
       Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or paid to             公平值是於計量日市場參與者於有秩序交
       transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between market participants             易中出售資產可收取或轉讓負債須支付之
       at the measurement date. The following disclosures of fair value                       價格。以下公平值計量披露乃採用公平值
       measurements use a fair value hierarchy that categorises into three levels             架構，此架構把輸入數據分為三個估價層
       the inputs to valuation techniques used to measure fair value:                         級用於量度公平值：

       Level 1 inputs: quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets for identical             第1級輸入數據：本集團可於計量日獲取
       assets or liabilities that the Group can access at the measurement date.               之相同資產或負債於活躍市場之報價（未
                                                                                              經調整）。

       Level 2 inputs: inputs other than quoted prices included within level 1 that           第2級輸入數據：就資產或負債可直接或
       are observable for the asset or liability, either directly or indirectly.              間接觀察取得的除第1級之報價之外之輸
                                                                                              入數據。

       Level 3 inputs: unobservable inputs for the asset or liability.                        第3級輸入數據：資產或負債之不可觀察
                                                                                              輸入數據。

       The Group’s policy is to recognise transfers into and transfers out of any             本集團之政策是於引起轉移之事件或情況
       of the three levels as of the date of the event or change in circumstances             變化發生之日確認任何三個等級之轉入或
       that caused the transfer.                                                              轉出。

       (b)    Segment profit or loss                                                                   (b)       分部損益

              The segment profit or loss for the reportable segments provided to                                 截至二零二三年及二零二二年三月
              the chief operating decision maker and reconciliation to profit/loss                               三十一日止年度，提供予主要營運
